@@shikigami/shikigami/serpent_boss
execute unless entity @e[type=ds:serpentboss,r=100] run kill @e[type=ds:serpentboss,c=1]
execute unless entity @e[type=ds:serpentboss,r=100] run function shikigami/shikigami/serpent_tame
execute unless entity @e[type=ds:serpentboss,r=100] run tag @s remove serpent_fight
@@shikigami/shikigami/serpent_boss_spawn
tag @p[tag=shikigami]  add serpent_fight
summon ds:serpentboss ~~~
tellraw @p {"rawtext":[{"translate":"text.serpent_boss_spawn.shikigami"}]}
playsound note.pling @p ~~~ 1 0.4
camerashake add @a[r=15] 0.2 1
@@shikigami/shikigami/serpent_spawn
event entity @e[type=ds:familiar_entity,r=1,c=1] serpent_spawn
camerashake add @a[r=15] 0.2 1
@@shikigami/shikigami/serpent_tame
tellraw @p { "rawtext": [ { "translate":"text.serpent_tame.shikigami" } ] }
tag @p add serpent_tame
scoreboard players set @p[tag=serpent_tame] ability5 0
playsound random.levelup @a ~~~ 1 0.5
playsound mob.serpent @a ~~~ 1 0.75

@@shikigami/shikigami/toad_boss
execute unless entity @e[type=ds:toad,r=100] run kill @e[type=ds:toad,c=1]
execute unless entity @e[type=ds:toad,r=100] run function shikigami/shikigami/toad_tame
execute unless entity @e[type=ds:toad,r=100] run tag @s remove toad_fight
execute as @e[scores={Deleter=1},type=ds:toad] run tag @p[tag=toad_fight] remove toad_fight
@@shikigami/shikigami/toad_boss_spawn
tag @p[tag=shikigami] add toad_fight
summon ds:toad ~~~
scoreboard players set @e[type=ds:toad,c=1] Deleter 2400
tellraw @p {"rawtext":[{"translate":"text.toad_boss_spawn.shikigami"}]}
playsound note.pling @p ~~~ 1 0.4
camerashake add @a[r=15] 0.2 1
@@shikigami/shikigami/toad_spawn
event entity @e[type=ds:familiar_entity,r=1,c=1] toad_spawn
camerashake add @a[r=15] 0.2 1
@@shikigami/shikigami/toad_tame
tellraw @p { "rawtext": [ { "translate":"text.toad_tame.shikigami" } ] }
tag @p add toad_tame
scoreboard players set @p[tag=toad_tame] ability2 0
playsound random.levelup @a ~~~ 1 0.5
playsound mob.slime.jump @a ~~~
@@shikigami/shikigami/totality

tag @s[scores={Energy=0}] add divinedog

execute as @s[type=ds:totality,scores={Disabled=0,move=0}] at @s positioned ^^^2.5 if entity @e[tag=!Frames,r=2.45,type=!item,type=!xp_orb,family=!damage,family=!projectile,type=!ds:projectile,type=!arrow,family=!despawncito,tag=!unselect,type=!armor_stand,family=!shikigami,family=!shadows,tag=!dog_tame,c=1] run scoreboard players random @s chance 1 2

scoreboard players set @s[scores={chance=1,Stun=0,move=1..}] Disabled 25
